<a id="node-0318"></a>
### NODE-0318 · Show accumulated Elder Grove growth and Tribute stock

**2026-11-04 · Fixed · Implemented · Local**

The Grove bar now follows saved cumulative growth instead of an unused cycle timer. Exact received/required counts remain visible below one percent; its Tribute shows stored components out of capacity.

- Preserves the adopted two-million-growth quota, paid Planter recipes, saved progress and simulation rules. The explanation states cumulative work, immediate awards, surplus preservation and full-Tribute pause.
- The prior UI-only playtest halt established a visibility dead end, not a confirmed simulation failure. A separate engine regression verifies paid Planter delivery across multiple funding batches, save/reload and a real threshold award.

Topics: Encounters · UI · Progression

Verification: 34 focused Vitest tests passed across groveProgress, productionVisuals and endgame; npm run build passed with the existing bundle-size advisory. Published playtest save remains paused and untouched. Local implementation only; browser visual QA and GitHub Pages publication have not been performed.

<a id="node-0316"></a>
### NODE-0316 · Apply adopted endgame research prices, freight growth and state-preserving relocation

**2026-11-04 · Changed · Implemented · Published by eca1c2d**

Implement seven-Core prices, finite Road/Landfill ranks, uncapped Train/regeneration lines and separate resource/Lake/camp movement permissions.

- Global access costs one each of seven Cores after first Final Core production; separate placement/connection lines follow the same independently rounded 1.5-growth curve. Keep one updating recurring entry and cap completed finite lines.
- Post-victory Train ranks add twenty capacity and one local/Road payload; Road research reaches five slots per face. Forest-inclusive regeneration adds the ordinary-researched baseline each rank.
- Use exact compounded Landfill totals with paid-fill credit, a one-Stone floor and rank-45 cap. Preserve unused fill stock when clearing. Legacy missing provenance uses a documented best-effort remaining-bill base.
- Allow eligible same-map resource relocation including cleared reserves; move camp/Tribute groups atomically and Lakes independently of Intakes. Retain IDs, stock, timers, wiring, undo and placement validation. Living Golems/corner challenges remain fixed; protected world features cannot be copied through templates.

Topics: Research · Logistics · Resources · Interface · Persistence · World

Verification: Full Vitest report: 699 passed, one pre-existing skip; TypeScript/Vite build and progression analysis passed. Focused engine tests cover conserved stock and selected endgame behavior. Browser fixture navigation timed out and error-page recovery was blocked by URL policy, so no successful visual/interactive browser QA is claimed. No normal-play endgame pacing or sustained waste-flow balance run; no live publication confirmed.

<a id="node-0315"></a>
### NODE-0315 · Add fixed-composition recycling with a hidden Golem recovery failsafe

**2026-11-04 · Added · Implemented · Published by eca1c2d**

Enable the Hydro-era Recycler together with conservative internal next-expansion shortage checks and sequential target-specific Golem rescue.

- Reviewed forty manufactured items use fixed standard constituent types, including fuel Wood, at most one of each solid type. Raw inputs return nothing; fluids are not recovered and waste/unmapped future goods are rejected. Two-second FIFO processing preserves complete returns across blocked ten-item buffers.
- The internal shortage relaxation counts paid research, stock, pending/committed output and accessible supply without exposing a player budget. Proven Iron/Copper/Stone deficits open a shortest route and administratively clear the target Golem, preserving quote/progress and Map Points. Wood is excluded.
- Persist one notice at a time and distinct rescue history for Destructive Testing. The proof is conservative rather than exhaustive; custom mods, Dev Sources and tutorials disable it. Broader normal-play coverage and achievement attainability require further validation.

Topics: Production · Resources · Research · Encounters · Persistence · Interface

Verification: Full Vitest report: 699 passed, one pre-existing skip; TypeScript/Vite build and progression analysis passed. Focused engine tests cover conserved stock and selected endgame behavior. Browser fixture navigation timed out and error-page recovery was blocked by URL policy, so no successful visual/interactive browser QA is claimed. No normal-play endgame pacing or sustained waste-flow balance run; no live publication confirmed.

<a id="node-0312"></a>
### NODE-0312 · Create, apply and share data mods through guided factory editors

**2026-11-04 · Added · Implemented · Published by eca1c2d**

Options now opens a Mods modal with category editors, base-game templates, local drafts, a change review, save-aware activation and copy/paste NODEMOD1 share codes.

- Guided forms edit existing resources/items, Production nodes including Extractors and Lake Intakes, Logistics/storage including Carts, world obstructions, research, recipes, map fields/reserves and connection/construction timings. Ingredients, yields, chance products, allowed recipes, socket types/counts/sharing, buffers and per-node cable limits are editable without JSON.
- Share codes encode versioned UTF-8 data with a copy-damage checksum. Strict validation rejects unsupported keys, invalid references and quantities, malformed codes and research dependency cycles before applying any changes. Scripts and new runtime behavior are outside the data format.
- Applied packages travel with saves and undo. Catalog activation retains cached research/build references and source-owned base templates; tutorials and clean factories use base rules. Playfield scenes refresh when a package changes. Active stock remains intact when capacities fall, and Cart total-capacity behavior remains enforced.
- Application and removal reject incompatible attached cables atomically. Reserve settings affect newly generated nodes/maps; build and travel settings affect future construction/shipments. Removing a package restores rules without rewinding stock, research or generated maps. Existing milestone triggers, scripted effects/caps and routing roles remain.
- A separate local paused factory verified Options to Mods, a named Wood-to-Timber draft, review/application, generated-code copy/reimport, damaged-code feedback, base-template recovery after development updates and Production-editor controls. No existing browser factory or save was used for these edits.

Topics: Mods · Interface · Production · Logistics · Connections · Recipes · Research · World generation · Persistence · Documentation

Verification: 19 focused mod tests pass. Full Vitest run: 699 passed, one pre-existing skip; TypeScript/Vite production build passes with the existing large-bundle warning. Browser interaction checked at http://127.0.0.1:4180/ in the default 1280 by 720 viewport. No full balance run, cross-device browser certification or publication.

### NODE-0297 · Implement paid industrial forestry, tier-scaled vehicle shipments and the first-Final-Core global gate

Fund 100 growth per 10 Wood/1 Iron Plate/1 Stone; add 600-Core tenfold forestry research and grouped growth. Ordinary vehicles ship 1/2/4 locally and across Roads, with global access unavailable before first Final Core production.

- Local launch/travel remain 400/1000 ms; Road dispatch remains five seconds. Partial quantities reserve actual units and refund in order on cancellation; copied planters receive no paid credit. Cart intake no longer adds an independent two-second bottleneck.

### NODE-0234 · Condense Options and shortcut node selection

**2026-11-03 · Changed · Implemented · Local**

Options uses two columns for the four preference toggles and moves button descriptions/flavor to hover help. Shortcut node assignment uses narrow columns of at most five names, expanding toward the roomier screen edge.

- Reorder top Options actions to Save / Load, Commands, Recipes, Tutorials, Journal. Shared action markup keeps callbacks and practice Save/Load restrictions, while descriptions and canonical dry flavor appear in existing hover/focus tooltips and accessible descriptions. Reset shortcut bars uses the same compact presentation.
- Group Wire animations, World grid, Always snap nodes to grid and Confirm deletion into two columns. Keep native checkboxes and preference writes. Use the available modal content height so all four fit at the standard desktop size; narrow screens retain two columns with wrapping labels and omit decorative toggle glyphs.
- The shortcut assignment menu uses 152-pixel icon/name columns with no category subtext and at most five choices vertically. Compare left/right space at the invoking slot, place and order columns toward the roomier side, and clamp the menu to the viewport. Short viewports may use fewer than five rows; horizontal scrolling and keyboard focus reach overflow columns on small screens.
- Retain selection, Clear Slot, close/Escape, unlock filtering and tutorial-disabled choices. Recalculate menu dimensions when the viewport changes without modifying saved shortcut-bar placement.

Topics: Interface · Controls · Shortcuts · Documentation

Verification: TypeScript/Vite production build passed. All 28 existing shortcut, preference, group and responsive-layout checks passed. With Dev research unlocks, verified 27 assignment choices split into columns of 5/5/5/5/5/2 with no subtext, both expansion directions, on-screen bounds, Clear Slot and actual reassignment, Escape, and keyboard access to the last column in a horizontally scrolling narrow menu. Screenshots saved. Viewport override reset, temporary QA tab closed, existing game/published/history tabs preserved. Layout-only changes; no new balance run or full simulation-suite rerun. Local only; not published.

<a id="node-0232"></a>
### NODE-0232 · Show Road flow arrows and move cargo directly without Road storage

**2026-11-03 · Changed · Implemented · Local**

Roads show a large flow arrow, use standard header icons and hold no inventory. Map Road hovers show only direction and item; Cart-family nodes use an icon header and the same shading dimensions as Roads.

- A large filled arrow below the Road header points toward the map edge for export and into the field for import. It derives orientation from the inward-facing socket, supports all four faces, appears in placement previews and updates when pair direction changes. Header symbols match the standard 21-unit node icon in a 33-unit plate.
- Cart, Truck, Train and legacy Chest visuals replace header title text with the existing centered storage icon. Their 42-unit shaded header matches Road geometry. Keep the four-by-five capacity squares, 128-unit footprint, inventory capacities, ports, serialized IDs and control-group markers.
- Minimap and expanded-map Road tooltips contain only a compass route such as H to W1 and the material name. Infer material from both ends, wiring and stock, with the last assigned cable item as a fallback when empty. Remove the Road transport label and verbose map names.
- Each export check moves one item directly from a connected upstream inventory to a connected downstream input, at 12 items per minute. Support local logistics routes on both sides, exact recipe sockets, shared or per-item capacity, real in-flight reservations, single-material and fluid restrictions, valid reciprocal roles, and Dev Source. No receiving or sending Road inventory is filled. Disconnected/full destinations leave cargo at its source, and missed trips never accumulate.
- Local cable animation uses cosmetic pulses and never reserves receiving inventory. Existing save cargo returns to connected inventories if space permits; any remainder is preserved in the shared pool. Pending Road deliveries refund their original sources before migration. Migration clears Road bags, sets capacity to zero and is idempotent; retired Fluid Road cargo retains its prior compatibility handling.
- Retain atomic paired placement, face-slot rules and one-way direction controls from NODE-0228. Direction changes still disconnect endpoint cables for rewiring; stock remains in actual inventories. Expansion dialogue and checks now wait for both Carts to be connected before shipping, rather than treating a Road endpoint as storage.

Topics: Roads · Logistics · Storage · Interface · Tutorials · Persistence · Testing

Supersedes: [NODE-0228](<2026-11.md#node-0228>)

Verification: Full Vitest suite: 619 passed, one existing skip. Final TypeScript/Vite build passed. Eight added regressions cover disconnected/full receivers, no catch-up trips, logistics on both sides, capacity reservations versus cosmetic pulses, Dev Source, reversal, pending-delivery refunds and save-load cargo conservation/idempotence. Updated earlier buffer-based Road and tutorial assertions to test direct delivery. Browser QA on a separate port-4175 tab used Dev research/map unlocks and normal placement/cable controls; verified arrow reversal, standard icon size, matching vehicle/Road shading and the final two-line H to W1 / Copper map hover. Screenshots saved. Main port-4174 factory and existing published/history tabs preserved; temporary QA tab closed. This was development QA, not a balance run. Local only; not published.

<a id="node-0228"></a>
### NODE-0228 · Validate facing Road pairs and toggle one-way flow from either endpoint

**2026-11-03 · Changed · Superseded · Local**

Roads create facing endpoints together only when both placements are legal. Clicking either endpoint opens a compact Road flow panel: click its direction card to reverse the pair, with 5 sec / item underneath.

- Retain atomic validation of both locations and both face-slot limits. Select the nearest intended map face first; if its neighbor is locked, reject placement instead of snapping to a different unlocked face. Valid pairs have reciprocal IDs, facing edges and opposite sending/receiving roles.
- Road clicks and configuration controls open the side panel. Its only content below the title is the clickable direction card and 5 sec / item, per the follow-up request; explanatory subtext, separate Send/Receive cards, material summaries and other text are removed.
- An explicit setRoadDirection operation updates both endpoints to complementary roles; reverseRoad delegates to it. An already-selected direction is a no-op. Reversing preserves stored items and disconnects both endpoint cable sets for rewiring, retaining existing direction-change behavior. No new face slots are used.
- Only export-to-import pairs dispatch across maps. Malformed double-export or mismatched counterpart references cannot dispatch; valid explicit direction selection can repair complementary roles. Legacy omitted reverse references remain compatible.
- Expansion copy explains paired legal placement and one-way flow. Its direction toggle is disabled during practice to preserve the required lesson route; normal play permits reversal from either endpoint.

Topics: Roads · Logistics · Interface · Tutorials · Testing

Replaced by: [NODE-0229](<2026-11.md#node-0229>), [NODE-0232](<2026-11.md#node-0232>)

Verification: Full Vitest suite: 611 passed with one existing skip. Final TypeScript/Vite production build passed. Twelve new Road regressions cover all four faces, side-effect-free previews, atomic local/remote obstruction and remote-capacity failures, locked-neighbor rejection, no fallback to a different face, counterpart identity, stock preservation, explicit direction selection from both ends, unchanged-direction no-op, save/load and single-direction dispatch. Browser QA at port 4175 in a separate paused factory used Dev research/map unlocks, placed a Road and visited its automatically created counterpart, verified opposite roles and control from each map, then rebuilt/reloaded and verified the final single card toggles H to E1 and E1 to H while only 5 sec / item remains below. Main factory and other tabs preserved. This was development QA, not a balance run. Changelog generated and checked. Local only; not published.

<a id="node-0225"></a>
### NODE-0225 · Add Road-unlocked Carts, global Truck and Train upgrades, and shared face slots

**2026-11-03 · Changed · Implemented · Local**

Wooden Chests become Road-unlocked Carts. Sequential Trucks and Trains research upgrades every vehicle to 40 and 80 items without replacement; all solid tiers are allowed from the start. Road faces support one, then two, then three endpoints through sequential upgrades.

- Retain serialized woodenChest/chest node kinds, IDs, wiring, stock, geometry and the existing two-second local output cycle. Normal new placement and copies require Road research; existing vehicles remain usable. Generated saved names and templates update to Cart, Truck or Train, with player-authored titles retained. Build, shortcuts and placement text use the current vehicle name.
- Use capacities of 20 for Cart, 40 for Truck and 80 for Train, shared across input/output stock and pending input reservations. Preserve saved over-capacity stock rather than deleting it; the existing four-by-five block display shows the filled proportion.
- Trucks costs 20 each Simple, Basic and Automata Cores after Road and Automata Core. Trains costs 40 each through Hydro after Trucks and Hydraulic Assembly. Completion upgrades all existing sector nodes and saved templates in place, and future placements start at the current level. Load, state replacement, developer research unlocks and undo reconcile the same level.
- The user explicitly rejected material-tier restrictions: every solid product and Core tier is allowed in the first Cart. New fluid inputs, including Water, Coolant and Conductive Slurry, are blocked in every vehicle level, including routed/legacy connections and Dev Source supply. Existing stored material is preserved.
- Road Channels 1 retains its 2-each-through-Hydro price and now requires Road plus Hydraulic Assembly, adding the second slot per map face. Road Channels 2 retains 130 each through Shiny and requires Road Channels 1 plus Ore Separation, adding the third slot. Both sides of placement count sending and receiving endpoints together. Reversal uses no new slot; saved excess pairs remain operational. Road speed remains one item per five seconds for every material.
- Expansion shows a persistent W1 sending Cart → Road pair → H receiving Cart route diagram, explicitly guides both real connections, and completes only when produced Wood arrives Home. Basics retains its buffer exercise with an explicitly practice-only Cart schematic loan; normal-game unlocks are unchanged by tutorials. Updated current catalog/docs and regenerated live research-consumption analysis for the additional research prices.

Topics: Research · Storage · Logistics · Roads · Tutorials · Persistence · Interface

Browser QA on separate port-4175 preview used a Dev research grant only to open isolated practice, then completed all 11 Expansion steps through normal controls: real Wood production, Road pair placement, sending Cart connection, receiving Cart connection and Achieved state. <a id="node-0211"></a>
### NODE-0211 · Check Research Center Cores immediately on project selection

**2026-11-03 · Changed · Implemented · Local**

Selecting or resuming research immediately consumes one complete buffered Core set when the Research Center is ready, credits project progress and starts the successful-check glow.

- Later payments retain the five-second base interval and all six research-speed ranks. A saved payment deadline prevents same-project clicks, cancellation, switching and save/load from bypassing that interval.
- An incomplete set consumes nothing and starts no glow. Existing waiting-for-material behavior remains after an unsuccessful initial check. Disabled, processing-paused, unfinished or locked-sector Centers do not charge on selection.
- Existing contributions are restored before checking. Only still-required Core types are consumed, so an immediately successful final payment can complete a resumed project. Unavailable project requests preserve current processing and feedback.
- Extends NODE-0159 with an immediate initial check while retaining its interval baseline; successful payments use the glow added in NODE-0207.

Topics: Research · Simulation · Testing · Persistence

<a id="node-0207"></a>
### NODE-0207 · Pulse Research Centers between successful Core payments

**2026-11-03 · Changed · Implemented · Local**

Each successful synchronized Core-set payment renews the blue completion flash for the full current research interval, so a continuously supplied Research Center keeps pulsing.

- Start feedback only when the entire required set is consumed, rather than on an individual Core arrival. Use the upgraded cycle duration and repeat the existing 480 ms blue completion pulse until the next check.
- Renew on each success; expire without another payment. Explicit cancellation or project switching clears prior feedback, while the final payment retains its full glow window.
- Timing follows simulation time across global pauses and save/load. Reduced-motion mode uses a steady soft highlight during the same window.

Topics: Research · Interface · Animation · Simulation

Verification: Full Vitest suite: 574 passed and 1 pre-existing skipped test. TypeScript/Vite production build passed. Browser visual check on a separate local preview at http://127.0.0.1:4175/ used Dev Source and developer research unlocks with Storage Connection Capacity: successive payments advanced project progress and the Research Center alternated between illuminated and unlit pulse frames. This was development QA, not a balance run. The live factory at port 4174 was preserved. Local implementation only; not published.

<a id="node-0140"></a>
### NODE-0140 · Add a universal Dev Source that automatically supplies connected inputs

**2026-11-02 · Added · Implemented · Local**

Dev tools can place a movable Dev Source with a multi-connection output that supplies each connected input with its required materials automatically.

- Available through Dev tools rather than the ordinary Build catalog. Placement, movement, deletion, save/load and undo use the existing node controls.
- Each semantic recipe socket gets its own material. A research CORE socket receives all outstanding required Core types. Bots, Stone, Forest Growth and any extant POWER input are inferred from the input family; deferred Power equipment stays deferred.
- An unconfigured production node may be wired to a Dev Source but receives nothing until its recipe is chosen. Recipe changes preserve valid Dev Source cables and update supplied materials.
- Extractors mine a virtual infinite reserve on their normal cycle, inferred from downstream demand. Lake Intakes accept a Dev Source upstream. Generic input families infer demand through local logistics or use an existing material, then Iron as fallback.
- Input buffers respect capacity and pending physical deliveries; cosmetic pulses do not create physical stock. Production time, research prerequisites, camp effects and visible obstruction boundaries remain in force. Ordinary sources keep their compatibility checks.
- Development QA only: these grants are not evidence about normal balance or progression. The live player game was preserved.

Topics: Controls · Interface · Production · Research · Connections · Playtesting

Verification: 558 Vitest tests passed, one existing skip, including 12 focused Dev Source tests; TypeScript and Vite build passed. Browser QA at isolated 127.0.0.1:4175 verified Dev tools placement, one source simultaneously feeding an Extractor and both Copper/Charcoal Smelter inputs, and completed Iron extraction/Copper Plate output. No browser console errors in that preview. Changelog generation/checks passed. Local changes only; no publication.

<a id="node-0129"></a>
### NODE-0129 · Unlock Tribute at minimum camp size and allow material changes with stock sacrifice

**2026-11-02 · Changed · Implemented · Local**

Tribute appears at the 10% minimum camp size. Clicking it opens a right-side resource chooser. Changing material discards other stored materials, keeps matching stock and resets the cycle.

- Fresh 25% camps need two successful checks: 25% to 15% to 10%. Growth removes Tribute, its stock and cables. Premature legacy rewards are removed on load even while paused.
- All reward nodes are named Tribute. Stock boxes remain; the progress bar and percentage are removed.
- The selector stays available with stored stock or pending deliveries. Already dispatched deliveries remain committed. Invalid choices do not destroy stock.
- Browser interaction verified clicking the node, the right-side chooser, retaining three Copper while discarding Iron, and changing Dwarven Mythril stock to Copper. The main game was preserved.

Topics: Combat · Logistics · Interface · Controls

<a id="node-0127"></a>
### NODE-0127 · Use visible obstruction borders and reserve maximum camp growth on spawn

**2026-11-02 · Fixed · Implemented · Local**

Lakes, Deep Holes, camps and Golems share their drawn outline with placement, cable blocking and camp encroachment. Generation validates maximum camp size and reserves its full maximum radius from resources.

- Camp placement validates its 100% visible body against field edges and other obstructions before starting at 25%. Resources reserve the entire maximum circle plus their full body and ordinary placement clearance.
- Runtime encroachment uses whole machine bodies and the union of camp polygons. Resources and other obstructions are protected; shrinking restores clear machines.
- Adaptive cable segments intersect visible outlines without an invisible ellipse halo. Obstructed existing cables stop routing and refund reserved transfers, then resume when clear.
- Generation rules apply to newly generated sectors. Existing saved layouts are preserved.

Topics: World map · Combat · Connections · Resources

Verification: 541 Vitest tests passed, one skipped; TypeScript/Vite build passed. Local changes only; no publication performed. Regression generation covered 1,210 sectors across ten seeds and validated all 300 camps at maximum size, including 150 Dwarven camps. Tests cover shorelines, small holes on long cables, whole-body encroachment and transfer refunds.
